Allocator evidence: replay capture /tmp/sol-x3-thread-cap-private reproduces 109/109; code/data/relocation sections equal the Ninja object. The all-nodes simulator reaches 12/12 only by moving @ temporaries. Named-only search reaches 7/12. u8 getCardCommandSlot is a real repeated message-field decoder and moves the mount slot into an inline return web, reducing 34 to 15 instruction differences. Capture /tmp/sol-x3-thread-cap-decoded reproduces 111/111 and its traced function equals the rebuilt object. Named-only search reaches 8/9 requested named colors, with file still r20 rather than r23. The mount-error and offset temporaries are r26/r23 instead of r20/r19. Both captures and want maps are /tmp/sol-x3-thread-*.

Palette proof for lever 10: target loadCardFileIcons has no explicit write to r6 before its format switch. At object offsets 0x1770-0x1790, format 3 branches at 0x178c straight to 0x17f0. The only palette assignments are li r6,0x400 at 0x17a0, li r6,0x800 at 0x17c0, and li r6,0 at 0x17dc, belonging to formats 1,2,0. Format 3 skips all three then reads r6 at 0x1818 or 0x1840, and again at 0x184c. A first reserved-format icon therefore consumes an unspecified incoming r6, exactly the explicit exception in levers.md 10. Any uninitialized palette trial is restricted to reconstructing that proven path; it is not used as a register-allocation trick.

## Saved best and final restoration

Compiled manual source attempts: {'cardThreadMain': 84, 'loadCardFileIcons': 40}. Every row records a fresh temporary object and exact-name objdiff report; all compiled candidates preserve the 28 exact sibling functions and 43-entry pool. Thread source search seed 100803 additionally ran 91 trials over 120 seconds, producing no gain from 99.50166%. Its private parser locates the definition instead of the earlier forward declaration. This search retained aggregate statistics only; individual search variants are unavailable, and no search candidate was adopted. Search output /tmp/sol-x3-thread-search.log; no search source was adopted.

cardThreadMain 34/301 -> 15/301 at equal 0x4b4 size, objdiff 99.16943 -> 99.50166%. Real u8 command-slot decoder changes inline-return allocation. Remaining 13 mount-register differences and two reply-packing differences are nonexact. loadCardFileIcons 290/512 -> 198/512 at equal 0x800 size, objdiff 95.80664 -> 96.34375%. Proven reserved-format no-assignment path, pointer-based NONE copy and direct image error return recover the equal-sized candidate; seven normalized structural differences remain at NONE address construction and the image error destination.

Icons capture /tmp/sol-x3-icons-cap reproduces 381/381 virtual registers. Parameter IDs 32-34 were pinned, compiler @ names excluded. Named-only search reaches 5/15 requested colors after 20000 steps, leaving parameter/derived-offset allocation unresolved. This bounded search is evidence of the remaining graph issue, not a proof that no source shape can match.

Fresh best object: unit 28/30 exact functions, 6600/9852 exact code bytes, 1496/1496 data bytes; both ctxdiff instruction counts equal with 15 and 198 differences. No exact match is claimed. Saved external diff /mnt/drive2/projects/wii-ipl-workers/_luna-runs/best/sol-x3.iplCardSequence.diff, SHA256 9bcc60f9f454b93f588d7723c8521ecb34f0fe5eb1cec778a98ea33b7c24e60b. It includes the lever-10 palette reconstruction with the proof above. Source restored before the requested final quick gate; no configure/header/other-unit change retained.

## Final gate

Requested quick gate passed after restoration. Full 43U build passes; independently rechecked DOL SHA1 26116613f624061ba99c8d1a299aaa6efa85670d. ### Round 3 xhigh

Fresh origin/main fetch under /tmp/wii-git.lock. Replayed the requested best, cardThreadMain 11/301 at 301/301 instructions, 99.56811% objdiff, pool identical at 43 entries. No source trials on loadCardFileIcons this round. optsweep.py ran once with optimization_level 0-3 and ppc_iro_level 0-1, reporting no leads for the unit. Fresh capture /tmp/sol-x3-r3-cap completed with unchanged compiler and flags on the private port.

Reply packing, indices 38-59: target and ours agree on the case dispatch, li r25,1, report call, queue load and send call. At 48-49 target is mr r4,r19; rlwimi r4,r25,8,16,23. Ours is li r4,256; rlwimi r4,r19,0,24,31. Before allocation B7 writes validity r34=1; B8 constant propagation replaces its read with 256 and inserts command byte into that word. Target needs the validity value read after the call and command copied into the reply first. Stored full +/-10 instruction windows in /tmp/sol-x3-r3-target.asm and /tmp/sol-x3-r3-ours.asm.

Mount offset, indices 47-70 and 134-154: target and ours have the same extraction, base addition and final mounted store order. The offset lives from B11 CARDMount to the B40 mounted store across every scan call. Target uses r19 for all three offset operands; ours uses virtual r51, @17706, colored r23. The allocator colors it after slot r54=r22 and before file r39, so it consumes r23 while the r19 color has not been requested yet. This is a lifetime/coloring dependency, not a scheduler inversion.

File index, indices 69-91, 112-139: same load/call/compare order, same six index instructions and loop backedge. Ours named file r39 receives r26; target uses r23. File and offset interfere. Both cross every scan call; file is colored after offset r51=r23, slot r54=r22, and globals r21/r27-31. Reordering the operation statements cannot remove that edge. A real inline boundary or value-web change must put the offset at r19 and file at r23 together.

Round 3 diagnostic correction: an inspection compile overlapped the final mount-index variants of the r3-shapes batch through the older shared /tmp trial destination. Those final mount-index measurements are advisory and cannot establish a gain; no result from that overlap was retained. Subsequent diagnostic compiles run only after the active batch has completed, and new candidates preserve their .o by label for inspection. The fresh Ninja/capture comparison uses independent paths and proves all allocated sections identical.

Scoped opt_propagation off leaves reply constant folding unchanged and moves slot/index colors, so it is not a viable fix. opt_common_subs off adds an instruction; scheduling off changes 89 positions; opt_dead_assignments off has no effect. None of those diagnostic pragmas will enter saved source. Entire scan-loop helpers add 6-7 instructions from return-status joins. Broken-file predicate helpers remain equal-sized at 13 differences; typed mount-index helpers tie 11 without changing the graph.

Behavior review rejects scan-loop return-status-with-check variants: they ran CARDCheck on the repaired-mount continuation, where the target deliberately skips it. They are diagnostic failures and will not be saved. Read-before-assign/report/return variants retain no source gain. Reply assignment shape can switch li+insert to rlwimi+copy, but command-first coalesces the insert into command r19 and substitutes the hoisted 1 in r29. Target instead copies command into r4 before inserting the loop-state register r25. Direct caller packet construction, mount operation helpers, and command compound/reassignment forms have no gain. Fresh named-only regsim uses pinned compiler @ temporaries and exact #51/#54 wants; its output is /tmp/sol-x3-r3-sim.txt.

Real command-loop and single-command handler helper boundaries preserved instruction count and pool but changed many scalar colors. Loop helper ties 55 differences; handler ties 46. Input packet bitfields tie 11 using the decoder and regress to 30 with direct slot access. Scope/loop-form combinations remain 15. No helper or packet source was kept.

Additional capture of the inline loop initially failed before reaching the requested function because --args did not enable sjiswrap automatically. socket.log proves compiler diagnostic 10430. Retried with --sjiswrap on; /tmp/sol-x3-r3-loop-cap2 completes. This graph numbers source inlinee scalars as @171xx. A private simulator only permutes the 11 proven inlinee source-scalar nodes 44-54, leaving compiler-created offset/slot temps pinned. It reproduces 111/111 and reaches 42/48 wanted colors. Source and original capture metadata remain unchanged.

Neither r20 nor r19 is in the pool yet. To recover target allocation, a real source boundary must make the case-12 r19 request occur before mount-offset coloring, or make an equivalent earlier request without altering any lifetime. The previous countCardBlocks helper trials declared s16 blocks and therefore added an extsh; round 3 now tests the target-proven full-width accumulator instead.

Source search seed 10080303 ran 600 seconds, 520 trial iterations, with no gain from 99.56811%. Each distinct compile has an attempts row and source hash, plus its exact diff and objdiff/instruction measurements under /tmp/sol-x3-r3-search/trials. The parser selects the function definition, and signed reinterpretations of message are disabled. No search candidate was adopted.

Full-width countCardTitleBlocks is instruction-identical in size, unlike the earlier s16 helper. Adding a real byte-local decode before masking the slot changes the inlinee numbering: slot locals color first at r22, title-block counters then request r20/r19, and the mount-offset temporary can finally take r19. Fresh byte-split helper capture /tmp/sol-x3-r3-blocks-slot-cap proves the mount offset and slot operands exact. The resulting 21-difference function needs file/scan allocation corrected; listing/delete indices can reuse the same file index because their live ranges are disjoint and they identify the same card-directory slot.

### Round 3 final reply investigation

The two-instruction best survived an additional packet investigation. Scoped optimization_level 0-4 and ppc_iro_level 0-3 on the packing helper itself change nothing. Scoped lower IRO on cardThreadMain preserves the r25 validity operand but adds mount-offset alignment and format-result copy instructions, loses the successful mount register allocation, and still coalesces the response into command. Real format helper/index spellings remove one extra copy but stay nonexact. No pragma was retained.

Command parameter const/reference/narrow types, enum validity states, structured outer loop/guard forms, report/set/send helpers with pointer or reference state, return-state helpers, packet constructor/setter members, scalar bitfield views and persistent response words all failed to reach exactness. Scalar views and persistent packet forms were diagnostics only and are not saved. Volatile-local diagnostics spill the flag to the stack and have no target proof; they are rejected. The continue-while control variant also changed the destination of a mount-loop continue, so it is rejected on behavior regardless of score.

The search starting at the 11-difference source ran 520 iterations, with 495 compiled unique measurements. The second seed 10080332 started at the 2-difference source and ran 305 iterations, with 284 compiled unique measurements. Neither produced a retained gain. Individual successful builds and failures are logged above with source hashes and per-function results; search diffs live under the named /tmp/sol-x3-r3-search paths.

Fresh owned-object checks on the saved candidate: pool identical 43/43; cardThreadMain 301/301 instructions, differences only 48 and 49, exact-name objdiff 99.73422%; all 28 exact siblings retained; code 6600/9852; data 1496/1496. The best is nonexact and is not accepted. The live private capture /tmp/sol-x3-r3-two-cap reproduces this object and shows constant 256 already in backend-00, before coloring. Valid-first gives li r4,256 plus low-byte insertion; command-first gives insertion into r19 from the hoisted 1 at r29, followed by a copy to r4. The target needs a copy to r4 first and insertion from persistent validity r25.

The fixed mount/file dependencies are now the actual source byte-slot decoder, full-width title-block counter, isolated listing loop and deletion operation. Their inlinee locals request saved colors before the byte-offset temporary, placing the offset at r19, scan status at r20, and file indexes at r23. Declaring scanResult before file in the caller preserves those colors. No dummy, out-parameter carrier, uninitialized value, assembly, or register hint was added this round. Inherited icon edits from the requested starting diff were preserved without experimentation; they remain outside this round's cardThreadMain-only scope.

Round 4 source result: 65 scored candidates including replay; no new exact function. Fresh private captures for the replay and command-first reply prove the reply temporary r72 is coalesced into command r32, and constant propagation replaces validState r34 with the hoisted one r71. Command/valid parameter constness and references, actual message-byte input, queue parameters, aggregate initialization, pack-return helpers, report argument assignment and update/report/send boundaries did not fix it. No declaration-order search can restore a variable read removed before allocation. Scoped IRO 1 diagnostic adds two instructions and is discarded.

Plain reverse locals reproduce every register, all three CARDDir offsets and the file-name offset, at equal 301 instructions. The address-taken scalar message is at r1+8 instead of target r1+0x10, and compareName is at r1+0xC instead of target r1+8. Six declaration orders, nested scopes, a real broken-file helper, native OSMessage and meaningful word/byte union views did not change that ordering. Explicit array-element addresses, char/s8/halfword comparison storage and natural initializers did not help. Named company/game pointers add instructions and are rejected. The final source uses plain reverse declarations and the readable counting/listing/deletion helpers, retains the best value-equivalent packing spelling, and discards all icon edits and diagnostic variants.

Integrity cleanup is retained under the task-specific instruction even though cardThreadMain is not exact: 2/301 incoming carrier candidate -> 13/301 clean candidate, 99.69768%; 11 stack-address differences and the original two reply-packing differences. Every one of the 28 exact sibling functions stays exact. The cleanup supersedes the generic restore-all-nonexact-source rule for this round. No loadCardFileIcons source change is retained. No configure/header/shared-source edits, new comments, assembly, padding, carrier, dummy value or forbidden pragma is added. Final gate below will validate this retained source, not a restored baseline.

Round-e best ordinary source combines separate company[2] and gameName[4] comparison buffers, with the initialized-palette icon reconstruction. The two comparison arrays have real CARD metadata widths; all six zero stores and both memcmp ranges are unchanged. This reaches all target stack offsets without a carrier. No palette value is uninitialized: the loop initializes paletteSize to zero, and the no-icon case inherits that zero. The animation helper performs actual animation metadata work and returns early on the same zero-speed condition. The unused imageReadDone label is removed.

Fresh combined-candidate Ninja build passed. Pool is identical at 43 entries. ctxdiff independently confirms cardThreadMain 301/301, diffs 2, and loadCardFileIcons 512/512, diffs 219. Exact-name objdiff is 99.73422% and 96.31445%, respectively. Unit remains 28/30 exact functions, 6600/9852 exact code bytes, 1496/1496 exact data bytes, with no exact sibling drop. All data sections remain 100%. No exact match is claimed.

Fresh icon capture /tmp/sol-x3-e-icon-private-capture matches the separately compiled function and every allocated section. The simulator reproduces 381/381 virtual registers. Stock search moves compiler temporaries and reaches 15/16 wanted colors; that is not realizable as declaration order. A private named-only search freezes parameter and compiler-temporary numbering and reaches 6/16 after 5000 steps. The outstanding problem needs a different source shape, particularly early generated image/slot address lifetimes; parameter copies, const views and top declarations did not fix it. Target/source palette blocks also differ, despite equal total size. No simulator-only permutation is kept. The official tracer output-path guard and shared-port queue were handled with the already-validated private-port transport; compiler and debugger code-generation settings are unchanged.

Best ordinary combined source saved to _luna-runs/best/sol-x3.iplCardSequence.diff, SHA256 87d9061e13a8aa8797ff045ec0a5472b8017f45ae696c93fa5891c6e5d970e85. Incoming diff backed up at /tmp/sol-x3-e-incoming.diff. Under the nonexact handoff rule, restore experimental source changes and retain only the required icon carrier removal, with ordinary declaration order reproducing sector r1+0xC, comment sector r1+8 and CARDFileInfo r1+0x10. The final gate certifies that retained cleanup, not the external partial candidate.

Round-e experimental source is restored. Retained source removes only CardSequenceFileData and its local. member accesses, using three ordinary locals. No header, configure, helper, packet or thread change is retained. External best has the explicit initialized palette and no carrier. Existing untracked logs remain untouched.

### Round e final gate and handoff

76 successful icon variants and 46 successful thread variants were measured, plus six failed icon-copy compilations corrected above. No new exact function. Retained cleanup compiles to identical allocated and relocation sections compared with a fresh compilation of the original carrier-containing source snapshot. This is a pure source-integrity fix, with all ordinary stack offsets preserved and unchanged report percentages.

Final required gate ran once on the retained cleanup: GATE PASS. Full 43U build passes; DOL SHA1 independently confirmed as 26116613f624061ba99c8d1a299aaa6efa85670d. Unit exact functions remain 28/30; exact code remains 6600/9852; data remains 1496/1496. All four data sections are 100%, with zero baseline regressions, forbidden additions or readability warnings. Retained cardThreadMain remains 13/301, 99.69768%; retained loadCardFileIcons remains 436/512, 509/512 instructions, 91.33984%. External best is 2/301 and 219/512, both equal-sized and preserving all 28 exact siblings. The best diff applies cleanly against the baseline index.

Changed paths to commit are only src/scene/cardSequence/iplCardSequence.cpp and this attempts log. No push, PR, merge, rebase, other-worktree edit, configure change, new assembly, carrier, uninitialized palette or code comment. Existing untracked perm5 and swe2ifd0 logs remain untouched. Gate output is /tmp/sol-x3-e-gate.log. Next round should start from the external best and preserve the initialized palette, then investigate reply coalescing and the icon function address-temporary ordering.

### Address-taken local layout research

Standalone source /tmp/sol-x3-f-stack.cpp uses the unit compiler command extracted through ccflags.py, including GC/3.0a5.2, -align powerpc, -ipa file and effective -O4,s. Compiled 55 functions varying declaration order, call order, sizes 1-16, nested/disjoint scope, comparison-only versus mutable function arguments, byte array versus six-byte struct, and separate CARD company/game buffers. All object dumps are /tmp/sol-x3-f-stack-results.txt. Sizes 1/2 precede the word; sizes 3/4 share its bucket and follow reverse declaration order; sizes 5+ follow the word. Declaration and first-use order do not move the six-byte array before the word; nesting and struct type do not either. This supports allocation by small size buckets, then reverse declaration order within a bucket. The byte-load-only array is still materialized after the word. Separate company[2] and game[4] naturally occupy offsets 8 and 0xA and put the word at 0x10. The saved clean thread uses exactly these real metadata widths and independently reproduces all target stack offsets.

Scanned the live exact-function report and object disassembly for addi rX,r1,8, then inspected source and disasm_fn dumps. Exact is_valid_app_id in iplNwc24Manager uses an address-taken u32 appId at 8, u16 groupId at 0xC, and char[6] at 0xE. These are parameter homes, so this example does not by itself prove local ordering. Exact DoRegistration in iplUSBAP has a received message word at 8 and a result word at 0xC ahead of larger scan/SSID objects. Exact ATERMStartNetworkStack has message slots 8/0x14 but no local byte array, so it is excluded as evidence. Exact WDGetPrivacyMode has three-byte OUI arrays at 8/0xC/0x10, ieLength at 0x14, an address-taken IE pointer at 0x18, and readIE[8] at 0x20. This shows small byte arrays preceding words and the larger array following them. The standalone results isolate the local-size rule from those parameter/inline effects. No carrier or forced alignment is needed.

Fresh icon capture /tmp/sol-x3-f-icon-capture reproduces 381/381 virtual registers, and every allocated and relocation section matches the Ninja object. The first discrepancy is dir r31 versus r26. Coloring first claims r31 for dir, then r30/r29 for fileNo/slot, while the target reserves r31 for image base and r30/r29 for icon column/row. The two row/column and image row/column webs are created during the banner switch and coalesced across its cases; moving scalar declarations alone cannot assign their compiler temporaries. Read-only image-base/get-image/get-icon-state helpers and actual banner, image-read, comment-read and icon-format helpers were compiled. No improvement beyond the 219-difference seed yet. Cached IconState pointer/reference candidates reduce the function to 424 instructions and are rejected. Two first versions failed due to a real icon-loop local name collision; the corrected iconState versions are scored.

A private srcsearch copy fixes the shared tool's forward-declaration selection so mutations touch the actual loadCardFileIcons definition. Its first preparation edit matched the count helper's regex rather than the function regex and failed before compiling any candidate; corrected before the search. Every scored mutation has a row above and full source/digest in /tmp/sol-x3-f-search306b/trials.jsonl. No search source is retained without semantic and readability review.

### Round f retained candidate

Fresh thread capture /tmp/sol-x3-f-thread-capture matches every allocated and relocation section of the Ninja object and reproduces 124/124 virtual registers. Before allocation, B7 sets validState to one and reports it; B8 already replaces the shifted validState with the constant 256 before inserting command's low byte. Command-first packing instead coalesces the response into command and copies it after insertion. Eight new halfword/byte bitfield and explicit packet-field variants do not fix this. Five scoped IRO1 diagnostics on the separate metadata-buffer source remain 303 instructions and are discarded. No pragma or packet change is retained.

Further icon work includes banner, animation/format, image-read, comment-read, metadata, file-open and close boundaries; native pointer/reference views; address decoding; cached icon views; and a 120-second source search. The search measured 89 candidates plus its starting object, with no score improvement while preserving every starting function score. Its complete rejected sources are outside the repository. Helpers grouping actual file operations are allowed, but none improved on the clean 219-difference seed. There is no compiler-simulator-only permutation, carrier, uninitialized palette, new assembly, raw offset cast, dummy object, definition/header change or new code comment in the retained diff.

Under this round's explicit authorization, retain and commit the clean previous-round seed: thread uses real company[2]/gameName[4] metadata comparison buffers, and the icons function uses the actual animation helper, initialized palette and target-shaped read-error control flow. Every byte comparison and zero store is preserved. The palette is initialized for every reserved-format path. CARDGetSectorSize returns zero on success; the explicit image-success assignment matches target behavior when the image byte count is zero. No new exact match is claimed.

Unit stays 28/30 exact functions, code 6600/9852 and data 1496/1496, with every data section at 100%. Unit fuzzy improves 98.16281% to 99.20138%. Zero baseline regressions, new forbidden patterns and readability warnings. The unit remains NonMatching and unlinked; no whole-unit completion is claimed.

An independent baseline source compilation confirms thread 13/301 and icons 436/512, with the latter initially 509/512 instructions. Thus the retained changes recover all thread stack offsets and the target icon instruction count. Continued round-f trials did not beat the incoming clean 2/219 seed. The two thread reply instructions remain blocked by constant propagation/copy coalescing; icon allocation and palette-block placement remain unresolved.
